Subproblem solver details:
Input fixed核 kernel `k` (length n) nonnegative sum1, target sequence y length n. Minimize max cross_conv(k, y) over y simplex.
Cross_conv length 2n-1. Objective logsumexp(β c). Gradient wrt y: β * B_k^T p, where p=softmax(β c). B_k^T p[j]=Σ_i k_i p_{i+j} (if c_k=Σ_i k_i y_{k-i}; derivative wrt y_j = k_{k-j}. Sum over k: Σ_i k_i p_{i+j}). Same as corr(k,p) but no factor 2. If k fixed, compute via FFT.
Softmax gradient as above.

Softmax value/grad top-level for self:
```
def lse_self(b,beta):
    a=softmax_from_b(b)
    M=fft_len(a)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,M)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,M)[:2*n-1]
    z=beta*c; zmax=z.max(); ez=np.exp(z-zmax); p=ez/ez.sum()
    val=(zmax+np.log(ez.sum()))/beta
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,M)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P,M)[:n]
    grad_a=2*beta*corr
    grad_b=a*grad_a - a*np.dot(a,grad_a)
    return val,grad_b
```
Wait derivative c wrt a =? We used corr = Σ_i a_i p_{i+j}, and grad_c=2*corr. Yes `grad_a=2*beta*corr`.
But `np.dot(a,grad_a)` is sum a_i * ∂h/∂a_i = β * a·2corr. Since h not homogeneous; formula for softmax gradient correct. If h were homogeneous degree 2, dot=2h maybe, but okay.

```
    c=irfft(A*X,M)[:2n-1]
    ... p
    val=(zmax+logsum)/beta
    P=rfft(p,M)
    deriv=irfft(np.conj(A)*P,M)[:n]  # A_x? derivative of c wrt x = kernel*p sum =? conj(A)*P gives corr(kernel,p) = Σ_i kernel_i p_{i+j}. correct.
    grad_x=beta*deriv
    grad_b=x*grad_x - x*np.dot(x,grad_x)
    return val,grad_b
```

Let's detail random local search:
```
def random_polish(a, rng, n_iters, base_sigma):
    b=np.log(a+eps)
    best_b=b; best_sc=score(a)
    for it:
       sigma = base_sigma/(1+it/100)
       trial = b + sigma*rng.standard_normal(n)
       # optionally keep mean? softmax heavy okay
       trial_x = softmax(trial)
       sc = exact_score_norm(trial_x) # already normalized
       if sc < best_sc:
           best_sc=sc; best_b=trial; best_x=trial_x
       # If improvement, maybe run small self LBFGS with beta high? (outside)
    return softmax(best_b), best_sc
```
But exact_score normalizes; okay. Need avoid too many Python overhead? 10k loops okay.

Detailed functions:

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution
import time

EPS = 1e-14

def max_power_two(m):
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length() # if m>0

def self_conv(a):
    n=a.size
    m=max_power_two(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*A,m)[:2*n-1]

def cross_conv(a,b):
    n=len(a)
    m=max_power_two(2*n-1)  # if both length n
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,m); B=np.fft.rfft(b,m)
    return np.fft.irfft(A*B,m)[:2*n-1]

def corr_kernel_p(kernel,p): # kernel len n, p len 2n-1
    n=kernel.size
    m=max_power_two(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(kernel,m)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,m)
    return np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P,m)[:n]
```
For cross derivative `corr_kernel_p(kernel,p)` as above. For self derivative same but kernel=a.

Normalize:
```
def normalize_sum(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    x=np.clip(x,0,1000)
    s=x.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return np.ones(len(x))/len(x)
    return x/s
```

`softmax_row(b)`: for 1D.
```
def softmax_1d(b):
    b=b-np.max(b)
    e=np.exp(b)
    s=e.sum()
    if s==0 or not np.isfinite(s): return np.ones(len(b))/len(b)
    return e/s
```
For batch:
```
def softmax_batch(B):
    B=B - B.max(axis=1,keepdims=True)
    E=np.exp(B)
    S=E.sum(axis=1,keepdims=True)
    # handle
    return E/np.where(S>0,S,1)
```

Exact score normalized:
```
def exact_score_norm(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-12: return np.inf
    a=a/s
    n=a.size
    m=next_power_two(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,m)
    return float(2*n*c[:2*n-1].max())
```
Batch:
```
def exact_score_batch(A_rows): # rows sum maybe 1? normalize
    B=A_rows.shape[0]; n=A_rows.shape[1]
    # normalize each row: S=sum axis1; replace small
    S=A_rows.sum(axis=1,keepdims=True)
    M=np.where(S>1e-12,S,1)
    X=np.clip(A_rows/M,0,1000)
    m=next_power_two(2*n-1)
    pad=np.zeros((B,m))
    pad[:,:n]=X
    A=np.fft.rfft(pad,m,axis=1)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*A,m,axis=1)
    return 2*n*conv[:,:2*n-1].max(axis=1)
```
Need ensure `A_rows` nonnegative. If a row all zero, S=0 set uniform? Could set X=1/n. In line search, not zero.

LSE self:
```
def lse_self_obj(b, beta):
    a=softmax_1d(b)
    n=a.size
    m=next_power_two(2*n-1)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,m)[:2*n-1]
    z=beta*c
    zmax=float(z.max())
    ez=np.exp(z-zmax)
    esum=float(ez.sum())
    val=(zmax+np.log(esum))/beta
    p=ez/esum
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,m)
    corr=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P,m)[:n]
    grad_a=2.0*beta*corr
    grad_b=a*grad_a - a*np.dot(a,grad_a)
    grad_b -= grad_b.mean()
    return val, grad_b
```
Could `np.dot(a,grad_a)` large? fine. For β large, val ~max.

But `minimize` with `jac=True` expects function returns scalar and gradient; returning tuple works. Good.

```
def lse_cross_obj(b, A, beta, n, m):
    x=softmax_1d(b)
    X=np.fft.rfft(x,m)
    c=np.fft.irfft(A*X,m)[:2*n-1]
    z=beta*c; ...
    P=np.fft.rfft(p,m)
    deriv=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A)*P,m)[:n]
    grad_x=beta*deriv
    grad_b=x*grad_x - x*np.dot(x,grad_x)
    grad_b-=grad_b.mean()
    return val, grad_b
```

Mix line search with batch:
```
def mix_line_search(a,x):
    ts=np.array([1.0,0.9,0.8,0.7,0.5,0.3,0.2,0.1])
    Z=(1-ts[:,None])*a[None,:]+ts[:,None]*x[None,:]
    Z=np.maximum(Z,0)
    sc=exact_score_batch(Z)
    i=np.argmin(sc)
    y=Z[i]
    S=y.sum(); if S<... return normalize
    y/=S
    return y, float(sc[i])
```
`exact_score_batch` normalizes itself. It expects rows nonnegative. Good.

Cross alternating function top-level maybe:
```
def alternate_pair(a, max_rounds, cross_beta, self_betas, time_deadline, verbose_tag):
   best_a=normalize(a); best_sc=exact_score_norm(best_a)
   b_self=np.log(best_a+eps)
   b_cross=np.log(best_a+eps)
   for round:
      if time exceeded break
      # cross solve
      x,cross_max=run_cross_lbfgs(b_cross, best_a, cross_beta, maxiter=..., maxfun=...)
      # maybe if cross_max > best_sc/(2n) * 1.05 still try
      a_mix, sc_mix = mix_line_search(best_a, x)
      if sc_mix < best_sc: best_a=a_mix; best_sc=sc_mix; b_self=b_from_a(best_a)
      # self solve(s) maybe with several beta
      b_self = b_from_a(best_a)
      for beta in self_betas:
         a_new, sc_new = run_self_lbfgs(b_self, beta, ...)
         if sc_new<best_sc: update
         b_self = b_from_a(best_a) # warm
      # update b_cross warm to log(best_a)
      b_cross = b_from_a(best_a)
   return best_a,best_sc
```

Fourier DE:
```
def fourier_b_from_coeffs(coeffs, n, K):
    iidx=np.arange(n)
    b=np.zeros(n)
    idx=0
    for m in range(1,K+1):
       ang=2*np.pi*m*iidx/n
       b += coeffs[2*m]*np.cos(ang) + coeffs[2*m+1]*np.sin(ang)
    return b
```
Function for DE objective:
```
def fourier_de_obj(coeffs, n, K):
   b=fourier_b_from_coeffs(coeffs,n,K)
   a=softmax_1d(b)
   return exact_score_norm(a)
```

```
def refine_sequence(a, deadline, max_n_full, tag):

    best_sc=exact_score_norm(best_a)
    n=best_a.size
    b_self=np.log(np.maximum(best_a,1e-12))
    b_cross=b_self.copy()

    # initial self

       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       a_new, sc_new = run_self_lbfgs(b_self, beta, maxiter=..., maxfun=..., deadline?)
       if sc_new<best_sc: best_sc=sc_new; best_a=normalize(a_new); b_self=np.log...
    # alternating

       if time exceeded: break
       # cross solve
       x, cross_max = run_cross_lbfgs(b_cross, best_a, cross_beta, maxiter=..., maxfun=...)
       # mix line search

       # quick self on best with current b

       if sc_s<best_sc update
       b_cross=b_from_a(best_a)
    return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def random_batch_polish(b, rng, iters, batch_size, sigma, deadline):
   n=b.size; best_b=b; best_a=softmax_1d(best_b); best_sc=exact_score_norm(best_a)
   iidx=np.arange(n)
   for it in range(iters):
      if time>deadline or enough: break
      sig=sigma*(1.0 - 0.5*it/iters)
      B0=best_b[None,:] + sig*rng.standard_normal((batch_size,n))
      # optional remove mean? not necessary
      A_rows=softmax_batch(B0)
      scs=exact_score_batch(A_rows)
      idx=int(np.argmin(scs))
      if scs[idx] < best_sc - 1e-15:
          best_sc=float(scs[idx]); best_b=B0[idx].copy(); best_a=A_rows[idx].copy()
          # optionally run quick self? outside
   return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def refine_sequence(a0, deadline, tag, max_rounds=12):
   best_a=normalize_sum(a0); n=best_a.size; best_sc=exact_score_norm(best_a)
   print tag initial
   b_self=b_from_a(best_a); b_cross=b_self.copy()
   betas=beta_schedule_self(n)
   # initial self maybe all betas
   for beta in betas:
      if time.monotonic()>deadline-60: break
      mf=choose_maxfun(n, deadline-time.monotonic())
      if mf<=0: break
      a_new,sc_new=run_self_lbfgs(b_self,beta,mf)
      if sc_new<best_sc: update; b_self=b_from_a(best_a)
   # cross loop
   cross_betas = beta_schedule_cross(n, rounds?) maybe use [max(500,n//2), n, 2*n]?
   for round in range(max_rounds):
      remaining=deadline-time
      if remaining < 90: break

      mf_cross=choose_maxfun_cross(remaining)
      if mf_cross<=0: break
      x, cross_max = run_cross_lbfgs(b_cross,best_a,cross_beta,mf_cross)
      # print cross max vs self max

      if sc_mix<best_sc: update; b_self=b_from_a(best_a)
      # self polish with a moderate/high beta

         if sc_s<best_sc: update
      b_cross=b_from_a(best_a)
      # if no improvement 4 rounds break? track
   # final high beta self and random polish
   for beta in betas[-2:]: ...
   return best_a,best_sc
```

```
def choose_maxfun(n, remaining):
    if remaining < 80: return 0
    if n <= 2048: base=12000
    elif n <= 8192: base=6000
    elif n <= 16384: base=3500
    else: base=2000
    if remaining < 240: base = max(300, base//4)
    if remaining < 120: base = max(100, base//10)
    return int(base)
```

Now, in LSE self, p length 2n-1 (lags), P=rfft(p,m). `corr=irfft(conj(A)*P)[:n]`. Does this compute corr[j]=Σ_i a_i p_{i+j} or Σ_i a_i p_{j-i}? Need verify with Fourier. Now, helper `resample_sequence(a, n_new)` using np.interp:
```
def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
    old=n=len(a)
    if old==n_new: return a.copy()
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n_new)
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    return b
```
If old has zeros, okay.

```
def run_self_lbfgs(b0, beta, maxfun=5000):
    b0=np.asarray(b0,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b0=np.where(np.isfinite(b0),b0,0.0)
    n=b0.size
    m=next_power_two(2*n-1)
    if maxfun<=0: return softmax_1d(b0), exact_score_norm(softmax_1d(b0))
    opts={'maxiter':max(100,maxfun//2),'maxfun':int(maxfun),'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-11,'maxls':20,'m':10}
    try:
       res=minimize(lse_self_obj,b0,args=(beta,n,m),method='L-BFGS-B',jac=True,options=opts)
       b_res=res.x if hasattr(res,'x') else b0
    except Exception:
       b_res=b0
    a=softmax_1d(b_res)
    return a, exact_score_norm(a)
```
`gtol` 1e-11 maybe. For high beta, need small but okay. If maxfun large, gtol maybe 1e-10. Use 1e-10 to avoid too many.

Detailed `refine_sequence` code:
```
def refine_sequence(a0, deadline, tag, rng, max_rounds=8, do_final_polish=True):
    best_a = normalize_sum(a0); n=best_a.size; best_sc=exact_score_norm(best_a)
    print(f"[{tag}] start n={n} score={best_sc:.8f}")
    b_self = b_from_a(best_a)
    b_cross = b_self.copy()
    self_betas = beta_schedule_self(n)
    # initial self: use first three betas
    for idx,beta in enumerate(self_betas):
        rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if rem < 80: break
        mf=choose_self_maxfun(n,rem)
        if mf<=0: break
        t0=time.monotonic()
        a_new,sc_new=run_self_lbfgs(b_self,beta,mf)
        elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
        if sc_new<best_sc-1e-15:
            best_sc=sc_new; best_a=normalize_sum(a_new); b_self=b_from_a(best_a)
            print(...)
        else:
            # still maybe keep b_self from res? If res not better but b_res might be useful? use if finite?
            if sc_new <= best_sc + 1e-9:
                # perhaps not update best but b_self=res? We can't get b back. Could relog a_new (same). Use a_new if finite and closer? no.
                pass
        # optionally update b_self to b_from_a(best_a) (warm from best)
    # cross loop
    cross_betas = cross_beta_schedule(n)
    no_improve=0
    for r in range(max_rounds):
        rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if rem < 180: break
        cb = cross_betas[min(r,len(cross_betas)-1)]
        mf=choose_cross_maxfun(n,rem)
        if mf<=0: break
        # if no improve, perturb kernel
        kernel = best_a.copy()
        if no_improve >= 2:
            noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
            kernel = best_a * np.exp(0.03*noise)
            kernel = normalize_sum(kernel)
            b_cross = b_from_a(kernel)
        else:
            b_cross = b_from_a(best_a)  # or warm previous? use previous x maybe; we don't store? We can keep b_cross from last.
        t0=time.monotonic()
        x, cross_max = run_cross_lbfgs(b_cross, kernel, cb, mf)
        elapsed...
        # optional if cross_max not significantly below self max, still mix
        a_mix, sc_mix = mix_line_search(best_a, x)
        improved = sc_mix < best_sc - 1e-15
        if improved:
            best_sc=sc_mix; best_a=normalize_sum(a_mix); b_self=b_from_a(best_a); b_cross=b_from_a(best_a); no_improve=0; print
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            # maybe keep b_cross as res x for next? We have x -> b_cross=b_from_a(x)
            b_cross = b_from_a(x)
        # self polish if time
        if rem > 240:
            beta = self_betas[min(r+1,len(self_betas)-1)]
            mf2=choose_self_maxfun(n, rem- elapsed)
            a_s, sc_s = run_self_lbfgs(b_from_a(best_a), beta, mf2)
            if sc_s < best_sc -1e-15: update, reset no_improve
        if no_improve >=5 and r<max_rounds-1:
            # random perturb best and self quick
            try ...
    if do_final_polish and time: final_high_self + random
    return best_a,best_sc
```

Gradient `grad_b` after softmax formula. Let's double-check for cross: `deriv` computed as `irfft(conj(A)*P)[:n]` where A is kernel rfft and P is p rfft. We derived derivative of c wrt x = Σ_i kernel_i p_{i+j}. If A is kernel, P p, conj(A)*P gives delta? Test: kernel delta at r, p at s. conj(A)P=e^{iωr-iωs}; ifft δ_{j=s-r}. That means deriv[j]=1 if j=s-r, i.e., kernel_i? We want derivative wrt x_j for c_k when kernel fixed. c_k=Σ_i kernel_i x_{k-i}. Derivative wrt x_j = Σ_k p_k kernel_{k-j}. Let kernel delta at r => r=k-j => k=j+r. Sum over k p_k δ_{k-j,r}=p_{j+r}. So deriv[j]=p_{j+r}. Our choi gives δ_{j=s-r} => j=s-r => p_s at x_{s-r}. That's p_{j+r}. Yes correct. For r=0, derivative[p lag k]=p_j; choi with kernel delta0: deriv[j]=p_j. Good. For earlier self a at r, p at s, derivative wrt a_j includes 2p_{j+r}? For self derivative 2a shifted; if a delta r, c_k includes 2 p? It matches.

Now, LSE self gradient: `corr=irfft(conj(A)*P)` for kernel a. `grad_a=2β corr`. Good.

Let's define `choose_maxfun(n, rem, kind)`:
```
def choose_maxfun(n, rem):
    if rem < 90: return 0
    if n <= 2048: base=10000
    elif n <= 4096: base=8000
    elif n <= 8192: base=6000
    elif n <= 12288: base=4000
    else: base=2500
    if rem < 240: base=max(200, base//4)
    if rem < 120: base=max(50, base//8)
    return int(base)
```

Now, fill in details.

`softmax_1d`:
```
def softmax_1d(b):
    b=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64)
    if b.size==0: return np.array([])
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
       b=np.where(np.isfinite(b),b,0.0)
    b=b - b.max()
    e=np.exp(b)
    s=e.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return np.ones(b.size)/b.size
    return e/s
```
`softmax_batch` similar but if row all inf? Use np.where finite.

`exact_score_batch`: Need handle B*n large memory. Use `pad = np.zeros((B,m), dtype=np.float64); pad[:, :n] = X`. Then rfft axis=1. But numpy rfft on 2D array with axis=1 works. Good.
```
def exact_score_batch(A_rows):
    A_rows=np.asarray(A_rows,dtype=np.float64)
    if A_rows.ndim==1: A_rows=A_rows[None,:]
    B,n=A_rows.shape
    S=A_rows.sum(axis=1)
    X=np.clip(A_rows,0,1000)
    zero=S<=1e-12
    if zero.any():
       X[zero]=1.0/n
    else:
       X=X/S[:,None]
    m=next_power_two(2*n-1)
    pad=np.zeros((B,m),dtype=np.float64)
    pad[:,:n]=X
    A=np.fft.rfft(pad,m,axis=1)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(A*A,m,axis=1)
    return 2.0*float(n)*conv[:,:2*n-1].max(axis=1)
```
But for rows not zero, X initially clipped, then divided by S. For zero rows, X set uniform after division? We need logic:
```
X=np.clip(A_rows,0,1000)
S=X.sum(axis=1)
ok=S>1e-12
X[ok]=X[ok]/S[ok,None]
X[~ok]=1.0/n
```
Good.

`lse_self_obj`: Should return (val, grad). Use `esum` maybe `float`. If `esum` infinite? After subtract max, no unless exp overflow? exp(z-zmax) <=1, sum finite <=2n-1. Good.

`run_self_lbfgs`: We pass beta maybe int. If beta<1, set 1. maxfun int.

`run_cross_lbfgs`: If maxfun<=0, return softmax(b0), max(cross_conv(kernel,softmax(b0))). Good.

```
def refine_sequence(a0, deadline, tag, rng, max_rounds=8):
    best_a=normalize_sum(a0)
    n=best_a.size
    best_sc=exact_score_norm(best_a)
    print(f"[{tag}] initial n={n} score={best_sc:.8f} time={time.monotonic()-start?}") # no global start. Just absolute? We don't have start. Print without time or pass start. Could use time.time? Not needed.
    b_self=b_from_a(best_a)
    b_cross=b_self.copy()
    self_betas=beta_schedule_self(n)
    # initial self
    for beta in self_betas[:min(3,len)]:
        rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if rem<90: break
        mf=choose_maxfun(n, rem)
        if mf<=0: break
        a_new,sc_new=run_self_lbfgs(b_self,beta,mf)
        if sc_new<best_sc:
            best_sc=sc_new; best_a=normalize_sum(a_new); b_self=b_from_a(best_a)
            print(...)
    # cross loop
    cross_betas=cross_beta_schedule(n)
    no_improve=0
    for r in range(max_rounds):
        rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if rem<200: break
        cb=cross_betas[min(r,len(cross_betas)-1)]
        mf=choose_maxfun(n, rem)
        if mf<=0: break
        if no_improve>=2:
            noise=rng.standard_normal(n)
            kernel=best_a*np.exp(0.03*noise)
            kernel=normalize_sum(kernel)
        else:
            kernel=best_a
        # warm b_cross: if previous x exists? Use b_cross variable. If kernel perturbed, maybe log kernel?
        if no_improve>=2:
           b_cross_use=b_from_a(kernel)
        else:
           b_cross_use=b_cross
        x,cross_max=run_cross_lbfgs(b_cross_use,kernel,cb,mf)
        # exact score of x? maybe if x alone better than best? include line search t=1 already.
        a_mix,sc_mix=mix_line_search(best_a,x)
        if sc_mix < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc=sc_mix; best_a=normalize_sum(a_mix); b_self=b_from_a(best_a); b_cross=b_from_a(x); no_improve=0; print
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            b_cross=b_from_a(x)
        rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
        if rem>260:
            beta=self_betas[min(r+1,len(self_betas)-1)]
            mf2=choose_maxfun(n, rem)
            if mf2>0:
                a_s,sc_s=run_self_lbfgs(b_from_a(best_a), beta, mf2)
                if sc_s<best_sc:
                    best_sc=sc_s; best_a=normalize_sum(a_s); b_cross=b_from_a(best_a); no_improve=0; print
        if no_improve>=5 and r<max_rounds-1:
            # random perturb and self quick
            try:
               b_pert=b_from_a(best_a)+0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)
               a_pert,sc_pert=run_self_lbfgs(b_pert, self_betas[-1], choose_maxfun(n, deadline-time.monotonic()))
               if sc_pert<best_sc: update
            except: pass
    # final high self
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem>120:
       beta=max(self_betas[-1], int(2*n))
       mf=choose_maxfun(n, rem)
       a_f,sc_f=run_self_lbfgs(b_from_a(best_a), beta, mf)
       if sc_f<best_sc: update
    # random batch polish
    if rem>180:
       b=b_from_a(best_a)
       for sig, its in [(0.06,80),(0.03,120),(0.012,160)]:
          rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
          if rem<90: break
          a_r,sc_r=random_batch_polish(b, rng, its, 12, sig, deadline)
          if sc_r<best_sc:
             best_sc=sc_r; best_a=normalize_sum(a_r); b=b_from_a(best_a)
             # quick final self
             rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
             if rem>120:
                a_f,sc_f=run_self_lbfgs(b, self_betas[-1], choose_maxfun(n, rem))
                if sc_f<best_sc: update; b=b_from_a(best_a)
    print end
    return best_a,best_sc
```

`random_batch_polish` needs `iters` maybe int. It updates b and returns softmax(b), score. At each improvement, update b. Use B=12. For speed, if n large, batch smaller? We can choose batch in caller.

```
def random_batch_polish(b, rng, iters, batch, sigma, deadline):
    b=np.asarray(b).ravel(); n=b.size
    best_b=b.copy()
    best_a=softmax_1d(best_b)
    best_sc=exact_score_norm(best_a)
    for it in range(iters):
        if time.monotonic()>deadline-30: break
        s=sigma*(1.0 - 0.5*it/max(1,iters))
        B=best_b[None,:] + s*rng.standard_normal((batch,n))
        B=np.clip(B,-50,50)
        A=softmax_batch(B)
        scs=exact_score_batch(A)
        idx=int(np.argmin(scs))
        if scs[idx] < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc=float(scs[idx]); best_b=B[idx].copy(); best_a=A[idx].copy()
    return best_a,best_sc
```
Could random polish accept a row with slightly worse logsum but lower exact? It uses exact. Good.

Now, `fourier_start_search`: Maybe not used; but include.
```
def fourier_start_search(n,K,rng,starts,deadline):
   C,bounds=build_fourier_basis(n,K)
   best_x=np.zeros(2*K); best_sc=exact_score_norm(softmax_1d(np.zeros(n)))
   for _ in range(starts):
      if time.monotonic()>deadline-60: break
      x=np.zeros(2*K) if _==0 else rng.uniform(-2,2,size=2*K)
      try:
         res=minimize(fourier_coeff_obj, x, args=(C,n), method='Nelder-Mead', options={'maxiter':200,'xatol':1e-5,'fatol':1e-7,'adaptive':True})
         x2=res.x if hasattr(res,'x') else x
         sc=fourier_coeff_obj(x2,C,n)
      except Exception: sc=best_sc
      if sc<best_sc: best_sc=sc; best_x=x2
   return best_x,best_sc
```

```python
"""
Optimizer for the non-negative sequence objective

    score(a) = 2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2

The method is a full-dimensional iterative refinement tailored to the
summation-convolution (sumset) peak:

1. Load the parent sequence if available and normalize it.
2. Optimize the sequence with a softmax L-BFGS pass on a log-sum-exp
   surrogate of the peak convolution, using a progressive temperature
   (beta) schedule.
3. Alternate a convex cross-convolution subproblem:
       min_x  max_k (a * x)_k  subject to x >= 0, sum x = 1.
   This is the same LP direction used by the previous state-of-art
   approach, but it is solved directly with a smooth softmax L-BFGS
   subproblem instead of approximate primal FISTA.
4. Mix the returned direction back into the current sequence using an
   exact-score line search.
5. Periodically perturb the kernel to escape local basins.
6. Finish with high-temperature LSE refinement and batched random softmax
   micro-polish.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None

try:
    from scipy.optimize import differential_evolution
except Exception:
    differential_evolution = None


def next_power_two(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m for m >= 1."""
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def normalize_sum(x):
    """Clip to valid non-negative range and normalize the sum to 1."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    x = np.clip(x, 0.0, 1000.0)
    x[np.isnan(x)] = 0.0
    x[np.isinf(x)] = 1000.0
    s = float(x.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.ones(len(x), dtype=np.float64) / len(x)
    return x / s


def softmax_1d(b):
    """Safe 1-D softmax."""
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = b.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    b = b - np.max(b)
    e = np.exp(b)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    return e / s


def softmax_batch(B):
    """Safe row-wise softmax for a 2D array."""
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64)
    if B.ndim == 1:
        B = B[None, :]
    B = np.where(np.isfinite(B), B, 0.0)
    B = B - B.max(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    E = np.exp(B)
    S = E.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    bad = ~np.isfinite(S) | (S <= 0)
    if bad.any():
        E[bad.ravel()] = 1.0
        S = np.where(bad, 1.0, S)
    return E / S


def self_conv(a):
    """Full linear self-convolution via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:2 * n - 1]


def cross_conv(a, b):
    """Full linear convolution of two equal-length sequences via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n != b.size or n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, m)
    return np.fft.irfft(A * B, m)[:2 * n - 1]


def exact_score_norm(a):
    """
    Exact internal score for non-negative a.
    The input is normalized to sum 1 before scoring.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a[np.isnan(a)] = 0.0
    a[np.isinf(a)] = 1000.0
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.inf
    a = a / s
    n = a.size
    m = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)
    return float(2.0 * n * c[:2 * n - 1].max())


def exact_score_batch(A_rows):
    """
    Exact score for a batch of sequences (rows).
    Each row is independently normalized to sum 1.
    """
    A_rows = np.asarray(A_rows, dtype=np.float64)
    if A_rows.ndim == 1:
        A_rows = A_rows[None, :]
    Batch, n = A_rows.shape
    X = np.clip(A_rows, 0.0, 1000.0)
    X = np.where(np.isfinite(X), X, 0.0)
    S = X.sum(axis=1)
    ok = S > 1e-14
    if ok.any():
        X[ok] = X[ok] / S[ok, None]
    if (~ok).any():
        X[~ok] = 1.0 / n
    m = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    pad = np.zeros((Batch, m), dtype=np.float64)
    pad[:, :n] = X
    A = np.fft.rfft(pad, m, axis=1)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m, axis=1)
    return 2.0 * float(n) * conv[:, :2 * n - 1].max(axis=1)


def b_from_a(a, eps=1e-12):
    """Log-space representation of a non-negative simplex vector."""
    a = normalize_sum(a)
    return np.log(np.maximum(a, eps))


def lse_self_obj(b, beta, n, m):
    """
    Smooth self-convolution peak objective and gradient in log-space.

    Objective:
        log(sum_k exp(beta * (softmax(b) * softmax(b))_k))

    The log-sum-exp value is returned scaled by 1/beta, so it approximates
    max convolution for large beta.
    """
    a = softmax_1d(b)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, m)[:2 * n - 1]
    z = beta * c
    zmax = float(z.max())
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    esum = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(esum) or esum <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64) / (2 * n - 1)
        val = float(c.max())
    else:
        p = ez / esum
        val = (zmax + np.log(esum)) / beta

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    corr = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * P, m)[:n]
    grad_a = 2.0 * beta * corr
    # softmax chain rule: a_i * f_i - a_i * sum_j a_j f_j
    grad_b = a * grad_a - a * float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    # remove flat softmax direction numerically
    grad_b = grad_b - grad_b.mean()
    grad_b = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_b), grad_b, 0.0)
    return val, grad_b


def run_self_lbfgs(b0, beta, maxfun=5000):
    """Run L-BFGS on the self log-sum-exp objective."""
    b0 = np.asarray(b0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b0 = np.where(np.isfinite(b0), b0, 0.0)
    n = b0.size
    m = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    init_a = softmax_1d(b0)
    init_sc = exact_score_norm(init_a)

    if maximize is None or maxfun <= 0:
        return init_a, init_sc

    beta = float(max(1.0, beta))
    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_self_obj,
            b0,
            args=(beta, n, m),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": max(100, int(maxfun) // 2),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "ftol": 1e-15,
                "gtol": 1e-11,
                "maxls": 20,
                "m": 10,
            },
        )
        b1 = res.x if hasattr(res, "x") else b0
    except Exception:
        b1 = b0

    a1 = softmax_1d(np.where(np.isfinite(b1), b1, 0.0))
    sc1 = exact_score_norm(a1)
    if not np.isfinite(sc1) or sc1 > init_sc:
        return init_a, init_sc
    return a1, sc1


def lse_cross_obj(b, A, beta, n, m):
    """
    Smooth cross-convolution peak objective and gradient in log-space.

    Fixed non-negative kernel has precomputed FFT A.
    Objective:
        log(sum_k exp(beta * (kernel * softmax(b))_k))
    """
    x = softmax_1d(b)
    X = np.fft.rfft(x, m)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * X, m)[:2 * n - 1]
    z = beta * c
    zmax = float(z.max())
    ez = np.exp(z - zmax)
    esum = float(ez.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(esum) or esum <= 0.0:
        p = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64) / (2 * n - 1)
        val = float(c.max())
    else:
        p = ez / esum
        val = (zmax + np.log(esum)) / beta

    P = np.fft.rfft(p, m)
    deriv = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(A) * P, m)[:n]
    grad_x = beta * deriv
    grad_b = x * grad_x - x * float(np.dot(x, grad_x))
    grad_b = grad_b - grad_b.mean()
    grad_b = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_b), grad_b, 0.0)
    return val, grad_b


def run_cross_lbfgs(b0, kernel, beta, maxfun=5000):
    """
    Solve / approximately solve:
        min_{x >= 0, sum x = 1} max_k (kernel * x)_k
    using a log-sum-exp surrogate.
    """
    kernel = normalize_sum(kernel)
    n = kernel.size
    m = next_power_two(2 * n - 1)
    b0 = np.asarray(b0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if b0.size != n:
        b0 = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b0 = np.where(np.isfinite(b0), b0, 0.0)

    x_init = softmax_1d(b0)
    c_init = cross_conv(kernel, x_init)
    cx_init = float(c_init.max()) if c_init.size else np.inf

    if minimize is None or maxfun <= 0:
        return x_init, cx_init

    A = np.fft.rfft(kernel, m)
    beta = float(max(1.0, beta))

    try:
        res = minimize(
            lse_cross_obj,
            b0,
            args=(A, beta, n, m),
            method="L-BFGS-B",
            jac=True,
            options={
                "maxiter": max(100, int(maxfun) // 2),
                "maxfun": int(maxfun),
                "ftol": 1e-15,
                "gtol": 1e-11,
                "maxls": 20,
                "m": 10,
            },
        )
        b1 = res.x if hasattr(res, "x") else b0
    except Exception:
        b1 = b0

    x1 = softmax_1d(np.where(np.isfinite(b1), b1, 0.0))
    c1 = cross_conv(kernel, x1)
    cx1 = float(c1.max()) if c1.size else np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(cx1) or cx1 > cx_init:
        return x_init, cx_init
    return x1, cx1


def mix_line_search(a, x):
    """
    Line search for a_mix = (1-t)*a + t*x over several t values.
    Returns normalized best mixture and its exact score.
    """
    a = normalize_sum(a)
    x = normalize_sum(x)
    n = a.size

    ts = np.array([1.0, 0.9, 0.8, 0.7, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.2, 0.1], dtype=np.float64)
    Y = (1.0 - ts[:, None]) * a[None, :] + ts[:, None] * x[None, :]
    Y = np.maximum(Y, 0.0)
    scores = exact_score_batch(Y)
    scores = np.where(np.isfinite(scores), scores, np.inf)

    if np.all(np.isinf(scores)):
        y = 0.5 * (a + x)
        y = normalize_sum(y)
        return y, exact_score_norm(y)

    i = int(np.argmin(scores))
    y = Y[i, :].copy()
    y = normalize_sum(y)
    return y, float(scores[i])


def beta_schedule_self(n):
    """Temperature schedule for self-convolution LSE refinement."""
    vals = [
        max(500, int(0.5 * n)),
        max(1000, int(n)),
        max(2000, int(2 * n)),
        max(4000, int(4 * n)),
    ]
    return [min(int(v), 80000) for v in vals]


def cross_beta_schedule(n):
    """Temperature schedule for the convex cross-convolution subproblem."""
    vals = [
        max(500, int(0.75 * n)),
        max(1000, int(1.5 * n)),
        max(2000, int(2.5 * n)),
    ]
    return [min(int(v), 80000) for v in vals]


def choose_maxfun(n, remaining):
    """
    Conservative maximum-function-evaluation budget for one L-BFGS call.
    adaptive to sequence length and remaining wallclock time.
    """
    remaining = float(max(0.0, remaining))
    if remaining < 90.0:
        return 0
    if n <= 2048:
        base = 12000
    elif n <= 4096:
        base = 9000
    elif n <= 8192:
        base = 6500
    elif n <= 12288:
        base = 4500
    else:
        base = 3000

    if remaining < 240.0:
        base = max(250, base // 4)
    if remaining < 140.0:
        base = max(80, base // 8)
    return int(base)


def random_batch_polish(b, rng, iters, batch, sigma, deadline):
    """
    Batched random micro-polish in log-space with exact-score acceptance.
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = b.size
    best_b = b.copy()
    best_a = softmax_1d(best_b)
    best_sc = exact_score_norm(best_a)

    iters = int(max(1, iters))
    batch = int(max(2, batch))

    for it in range(iters):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 20.0:
            break
        s = sigma * (1.0 - 0.5 * it / iters)
        B = best_b[None, :] + s * rng.standard_normal((batch, n))
        B = np.clip(B, -50.0, 50.0)
        A_rows = softmax_batch(B)
        scores = exact_score_batch(A_rows)
        scores = np.where(np.isfinite(scores), scores, np.inf)
        if np.all(np.isinf(scores)):
            continue
        idx = int(np.argmin(scores))
        if scores[idx] < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = float(scores[idx])
            best_b = B[idx, :].copy()
            best_a = A_rows[idx, :].copy()

    return best_a, best_sc


def refine_sequence(a0, deadline, tag, rng, max_rounds=8):
    """
    Main local refiner for one candidate sequence.
    Returns (sequence normalized to sum 1, score).
    """
    best_a = normalize_sum(a0)
    n = best_a.size
    best_sc = exact_score_norm(best_a)
    print(f"[{tag}] refine start n={n} score={best_sc:.10f}")

    b_self = b_from_a(best_a)
    b_cross = b_self.copy()

    self_betas = beta_schedule_self(n)
    cross_betas = cross_beta_schedule(n)

    # Initial self refinement with increasing temperature.
    for beta in self_betas[:min(3, len(self_betas))]:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 90.0:
            break
        mf = choose_maxfun(n, rem)
        if mf <= 0:
            break
        a_new, sc_new = run_self_lbfgs(b_self, beta, mf)
        if sc_new < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc_new
            best_a = normalize_sum(a_new)
            b_self = b_from_a(best_a)
            print(f"[{tag}] self beta={int(beta)} improved score={best_sc:.10f}")

    no_improve = 0

    # Cross-subproblem alternating loop.
    for r in range(max(1, int(max_rounds))):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 200.0:
            break

        cb = cross_betas[min(r, len(cross_betas) - 1)]
        mf = choose_maxfun(n, rem)
        if mf <= 0:
            break

        # If stuck, use a small perturbed kernel to find a different direction.
        if no_improve >= 2:
            noise = rng.standard_normal(n)
            kernel = best_a * np.exp(0.03 * noise)
            kernel = normalize_sum(kernel)
            b_cross_use = b_from_a(kernel)
        else:
            kernel = best_a
            b_cross_use = b_cross

        t0 = time.monotonic()
        x, cross_max = run_cross_lbfgs(b_cross_use, kernel, cb, mf)
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0

        a_mix, sc_mix = mix_line_search(best_a, x)
        if sc_mix < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc_mix
            best_a = normalize_sum(a_mix)
            b_self = b_from_a(best_a)
            b_cross = b_from_a(x)
            no_improve = 0
            print(f"[{tag}] cross round {r} beta={int(cb)} improved score={best_sc:.10f} "
                  f"cross_max={cross_max:.8e} elapsed={elapsed:.1f}s")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            b_cross = b_from_a(x)
            print(f"[{tag}] cross round {r} beta={int(cb)} no improvement "
                  f"cross_max={cross_max:.8e} elapsed={elapsed:.1f}s")

        # Self polish after mixing.
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 260.0:
            beta = self_betas[min(r + 1, len(self_betas) - 1)]
            mf2 = choose_maxfun(n, rem)
            if mf2 > 0:
                a_s, sc_s = run_self_lbfgs(b_from_a(best_a), beta, mf2)
                if sc_s < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc_s
                    best_a = normalize_sum(a_s)
                    b_cross = b_from_a(best_a)
                    no_improve = 0
                    print(f"[{tag}] self beta={int(beta)} after cross improved score={best_sc:.10f}")

        # Escape by a random log-space perturbation followed by short self refinement.
        if no_improve >= 5 and r < max_rounds - 1:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            mf3 = choose_maxfun(n, rem)
            if mf3 > 0:
                b_pert = b_from_a(best_a) + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)
                a_p, sc_p = run_self_lbfgs(b_pert, self_betas[-1], mf3)
                if sc_p < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc_p
                    best_a = normalize_sum(a_p)
                    b_cross = b_from_a(best_a)
                    no_improve = 0
                    print(f"[{tag}] escape improved score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Final high-temperature self refinement.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0:
        mf = choose_maxfun(n, rem)
        if mf > 0:
            a_f, sc_f = run_self_lbfgs(b_from_a(best_a), self_betas[-1], mf)
            if sc_f < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc_f
                best_a = normalize_sum(a_f)
                print(f"[{tag}] final self improved score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Final batched random micro-polish at several noise scales.
    b = b_from_a(best_a)
    for sig, its in [(0.06, 70), (0.03, 110), (0.012, 150)]:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 100.0:
            break
        its = int(max(30, min(its, max(30, int(rem / 2.0)))))
        a_r, sc_r = random_batch_polish(b, rng, its, 12, sig, deadline)
        if sc_r < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc_r
            best_a = normalize_sum(a_r)
            b = b_from_a(best_a)
            print(f"[{tag}] random polish sigma={sig} improved score={best_sc:.10f}")

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            mf = choose_maxfun(n, rem)
            if mf > 0:
                a_f, sc_f = run_self_lbfgs(b, self_betas[-1], mf)
                if sc_f < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc_f
                    best_a = normalize_sum(a_f)
                    b = b_from_a(best_a)

    print(f"[{tag}] refine finish n={n} score={best_sc:.10f}")
    return best_a, best_sc


def resample_sequence(a, n_new):
    """Resample a normalized sequence to a new length using linear interpolation."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    old = a.size
    if old <= 0:
        return np.ones(n_new, dtype=np.float64)
    if old == n_new:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return np.maximum(y, 0.0)


def build_fourier_basis(n, K):
    """
    Build a sine/cosine basis matrix C with shape (n, 2K).
    Constant offset is omitted because softmax is invariant to it.
    """
    n = int(n)
    K = int(max(0, K))
    C = np.zeros((n, 2 * K), dtype=np.float64)
    if K <= 0 or n <= 0:
        return C
    idx = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    for m in range(1, K + 1):
        ang = (2.0 * np.pi * m * idx) / float(n)
        C[:, 2 * m - 1] = np.cos(ang)
        C[:, 2 * m] = np.sin(ang)
    return C


def fourier_coeff_obj(x, C, n):
    """Exact score of a low-dimensional Fourier/softmax sequence."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    x = np.clip(x, -4.0, 4.0)
    b = C.dot(x)
    a = softmax_1d(b)
    return exact_score_norm(a)


def fourier_start_search(n, K, rng, starts, deadline):
    """
    Low-dimensional global-ish search over Fourier coefficients.
    Returns coefficient vector and score at length n.
    """
    C = build_fourier_basis(n, K)
    d = 2 * K
    best_x = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
    best_a = softmax_1d(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))
    best_sc = exact_score_norm(best_a)

    if d == 0 or minimize is None:
        return best_x, best_sc

    for s in range(max(1, int(starts))):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 60.0:
            break
        if s == 0:
            x0 = np.zeros(d, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            x0 = rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0, size=d)

        try:
            res = minimize(
                fourier_coeff_obj,
                x0,
                args=(C, n),
                method="Nelder-Mead",
                options={
                    "maxiter": 250,
                    "xatol": 1e-5,
                    "fatol": 1e-7,
                    "adaptive": True,
                },
            )
            x1 = res.x if hasattr(res, "x") else x0
            sc = float(fourier_coeff_obj(x1, C, n))
        except Exception:
            x1 = x0
            sc = np.inf

        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc
            best_x = np.asarray(x1, dtype=np.float64).copy()
            print(f"[fourier-coeff] search start {s} improved score={best_sc:.10f}")

    return best_x, best_sc


def simple_initializations(n, rng):
    """Generate a handful of diverse non-negative initial sequences."""
    inits = []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    inits.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) * 0.5)
    inits.append(0.5 + 0.4 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x))
    inits.append(0.4 + 0.3 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.2 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x + 0.7))
    inits.append(np.clip(0.5 + 0.3 * rng.standard_normal(n), 0.01, None))
    inits.append(0.3 + 0.7 * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2)
    inits.append(np.clip(0.35 + 0.35 * np.cos(3.0 * 2.0 * np.pi * x) + 0.2 * np.sin(5.0 * 2.0 * np.pi * x + 0.3), 0.01, None))
    return [normalize_sum(y) for y in inits]


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400):
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = 2400.0 if budget_s is None else float(budget_s)
    if total <= 120.0:
        total = 120.0
    deadline = t_start + max(60.0, total - 60.0)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(12245)

    parent = None
    try:
        if "height_sequence_1" in globals():
            p = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if p.size >= 16:
                p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
                p[np.isnan(p)] = 0.0
                p[np.isinf(p)] = 1000.0
                if p.sum() > 1e-12:
                    parent = p
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    # Use parent if available. If it is very long, still keep a reduced work copy.
    if parent is not None:
        if parent.size > 12288:
            parent_work = resample_sequence(parent, 12288)
        else:
            parent_work = parent.copy()
        a = normalize_sum(parent_work)
        sc = exact_score_norm(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
        print(f"Parent available: True len={parent.size} score={sc:.10f}")
    else:
        n_init = 8192
        cands = simple_initializations(n_init, rng)
        for idx, c in enumerate(cands):
            sc = exact_score_norm(c)
            if sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_a = c.copy()
        print(f"Parent available: False, using generated init score={best_sc:.10f}")

    fallback_a = None
    if parent is not None:
        fallback_a = normalize_sum(parent) if parent.size <= 12288 else normalize_sum(resample_sequence(parent, 12288))

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = np.ones(8192, dtype=np.float64) / 8192
        best_sc = 2.0

    # Main refinement on the best current length.
    n_main = best_a.size
    if n_main > 12288:
        best_a = normalize_sum(resample_sequence(best_a, 12288))
        n_main = best_a.size
        best_sc = exact_score_norm(best_a)

    main_deadline = t_start + max(600.0, min(total - 420.0, 0.82 * total))
    main_deadline = min(main_deadline, t_start + total - 240.0)

    best_a, best_sc = refine_sequence(best_a, main_deadline, "main", rng, max_rounds=10)
    print(f"[propose] after main: n={len(best_a)} score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Try a nearby/longer length if there is useful time.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 320.0:
        if best_a.size < 8192:
            n_try = 8192
        else:
            n_try = 10240
        if 16 <= n_try <= 12288 and n_try != best_a.size:
            seed = resample_sequence(best_a, n_try)
            sub_deadline = min(deadline - 80.0, t_start + total - 120.0)
            a2, sc2 = refine_sequence(seed, sub_deadline, f"len{n_try}", rng, max_rounds=4)
            if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-15:
                best_sc = sc2
                best_a = a2.copy()
                print(f"[propose] length {n_try} improved score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Low-dimensional Fourier seed, useful if we are near target or if no large progress.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 420.0 and best_sc > 1.50250:
        n_four = 2048
        K = 9
        x_four, sc_four = fourier_start_search(n_four, K, rng, starts=3, deadline=min(deadline - 460.0, t_start + total - 360.0))
        if np.isfinite(sc_four) and sc_four < best_sc + 0.10:
            C_full = build_fourier_basis(best_a.size, K)
            b_seed = C_full.dot(x_four)
            a_seed = softmax_1d(b_seed)
            sub_deadline = min(deadline - 120.0, t_start + total - 80.0)
            if sub_deadline - time.monotonic() > 180.0:
                a3, sc3 = refine_sequence(a_seed, sub_deadline, "fourier", rng, max_rounds=3)
                if sc3 < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc3
                    best_a = a3.copy()
                    print(f"[propose] fourier seed improved score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Use remaining time for direct final polish on the best candidate.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80.0:
        b = b_from_a(best_a)
        its = int(max(40, min(300, int(rem / 1.2))))
        a_r, sc_r = random_batch_polish(b, rng, its, 16, 0.012, deadline)
        if sc_r < best_sc - 1e-15:
            best_sc = sc_r
            best_a = normalize_sum(a_r)
            print(f"[propose] final random polish improved score={best_sc:.10f}")

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 90.0:
            mf = choose_maxfun(best_a.size, rem)
            if mf > 0:
                a_f, sc_f = run_self_lbfgs(b_from_a(best_a), beta_schedule_self(best_a.size)[-1], mf)
                if sc_f < best_sc - 1e-15:
                    best_sc = sc_f
                    best_a = normalize_sum(a_f)
                    print(f"[propose] final self LBFGS improved score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Fallback safety if verification fails.
    best_a = normalize_sum(best_a)
    out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]

    if "evaluate_sequence" in globals():
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = np.inf
    else:
        verified = best_sc

    if (not np.isfinite(verified)) and fallback_a is not None:
        best_a = fallback_a.copy()
        out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, x))) for x in best_a]
        try:
            verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
        except Exception:
            verified = np.inf

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.10f}")
    print(f"FINAL verified={verified!r}")
    print(f"Length={len(out)} Time={elapsed:.1f}s / {total:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)

    return out
```
